Night: Moreno vs. Albazi''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 246''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 104''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 2, 2024, at [[Rogers Place]] in [[Edmonton]], [[Alberta]], Canada.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/08/ufc-edmonton-dana-white-rose-namajunas-vs-erin-blanchfield-fight-card|title=Dana White announces UFC Edmonton with Erin Blanchfield vs. Rose Namajunas main event, more|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-08-15|access-date=2024-08-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's third visit to Edmonton and first since [[UFC 240]] in July 2019.\n\nThis event was the first to implement changes to the [[Mixed martial arts rules#Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts|Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts]] approved by the [[Association of Boxing Commissions]] in July which removed the ban of [[12–6 elbow]] strikes and deemed a fighter shall be considered grounded and may not be legally kneed or [[Soccer kick|kicked to the head]] when any part of their body other than their hands or feet is in contact with the ground (canvas).{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/10/ufc-news-mma-unified-rule-changes-edmonton-espn-elbows-grounded-fighter|title=New unified rules to make UFC debut in Edmonton with now-legal 12-6 elbows, grounded opponent changes l|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-10-29|access-date=2024-10-29}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] [[Brandon Moreno]] and [[Amir Albazi]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/9/3/24235265/brandon-moreno-vs-amir-albazi-set-as-new-main-event-for-ufc-edmonton|title=Brandon Moreno vs. Amir Albazi set as new main event for UFC Edmonton|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-09-03|access-date=2024-09-03}} The bout was initially scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Magny vs. Prates]], but was moved to this event shortly after.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/brandon-moreno/brandon-moreno-ends-brief-time-away-from-mma-set-to-return-at-ufc-vegas-100/|title=Brandon Moreno ends brief time away from MMA, set to return at UFC Vegas 100|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2024-09-02|access-date=2024-09-02}} They were previously expected to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Moreno vs. Royval 2]] earlier in the year but Albazi withdrew due to a neck injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2024/1/11/24035284/ufc-mexico-city-gets-new-headliner-after-amir-albazi-forced-off-card|title=UFC Mexico City gets new headliner after Amir Albazi forced off card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steve Marrocco|date=2024-01-11|access-date=2024-09-02}} \n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Erin Blanchfield]] and former two-time [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Rose Namajunas]] was originally scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/8/15/24220856/rose-namajunas-vs-erin-blanchfield-headlines-ufcs-return-to-edmonton|title=Rose Namajunas vs. Erin Blanchfield headlines UFC's return to Edmonton|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-08-15|access-date=2024-08-15}} However, in September, it was announced that the bout was shifted to the co-main event but remained five rounds.\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Alexander Romanov (fighter)|Alexander Romanov]] was expected to serve as the co-main event.{{Cite web|date=2024-08-15|title=Derrick Lewis added to UFC Edmonton vs. Alexandr Romanov|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/08/ufc-edmonton-news-derrick-lewis-vs-alexandr-romanov-fight-card|access-date=2024-08-20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} However, the promotion opted to split the pairing, with Lewis facing [[Jhonata Diniz]] and Romanov facing [[Rodrigo Nascimento]] instead.{{Cite web|author=Jed Meshew|date=2024-08-20|title=Derrick Lewis now fighting Jhonata Diniz at UFC Edmonton, Alexander Romanov faces Rodrigo Nascimento|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/8/20/24224829/derrick-lewis-now-fighting-jhonata-diniz-at-ufc-edmonton-alexander-romanov-faces-rodrigo-nascimento|access-date=2024-08-20|publisher=mmafighting.com}} After the weigh-ins, Lewis was forced to withdraw from the fight due to non-weight cut related medical issues, so his bout against Diniz was scrapped.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-11-02|title=Derrick Lewis out of UFC Edmonton, fight against Jhonata Diniz scrapped from card|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/11/2/24285974/derrick-lewis-out-of-ufc-edmonton-fight-against-jhonata-diniz-scrapped-from-card|access-date=2024-11-02|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent [[Sphere (venue)|Sphere]] \n|city= [[Paradise, Nevada]], United States\n|attendance= 16,024{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-306-post-event-facts-merab-dvalishvilis-streak-reaches-historic-status-in-title-win-vs-sean-omalley|title=UFC 306 post-event facts: Merab Dvalishvili's streak reaches historic status in title win vs. Sean O'Malley|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2024-09-15|access-date=2024-09-15}}\n|gate= $21,829,245\n|previousevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Brady]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Moicano vs. Saint Denis]]\n\n}}\n'''''UFC 306: O'Malley vs. Dvalishvili''''' (also known as '''''Noche UFC 2''''' and branded as '''''Riyadh Season Noche UFC''''' for sponsorship reasons) was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 14, 2024, at [[Sphere (venue)|Sphere]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|date=2023-12-20|title=UFC officially announces early 2024 schedule: Dates, locations, and venues|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-2024-schedule-toronto-miami-vegas-saudi-anaheim|access-date=2023-12-21|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-303-306-date-location-fight-card-news|title=UFC 303, UFC 306 dates and locations announced|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-02-25|access-date=2024-02-25}}{{Cite web|date=2024-06-27|title=UFC 306 at Sphere in Las Vegas rebranded as 'Riyadh Season Noche UFC'|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/06/ufc-306-riyadh-season-noche-ufc-rebrand|access-date=2024-06-27|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:Sphere-exosphere-on-Jan-26-2024.jpg|thumb|left|After hosting over 200 events in Las Vegas, this was the promotion's debut at [[Sphere (venue)|Sphere]], which opened in September 2023.]]\n\n=== Production ===\nUFC 306 was UFC's second event (after 2023's [[UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Shevchenko 2]]) to be held under the \"Noche UFC\" banner—a commemoration of [[Independence Day (Mexico)|Mexico's independence day]] in which all but one of the matches on the card (the main event between [[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]] and [[Merab Dvalishvili]]) featured at least one fighter of Mexican or [[Mexican Americans|Mexican American]] descent. As UFC's usual Las Vegas venue [[T-Mobile Arena]] had already been booked by [[Canelo Álvarez]] for his independence day fight against [[Edgar Berlanga]], UFC booked Sphere instead as a one-off event, marking the venue's first live sporting event.{{cite web |date=26 July 2024 |title=Alvarez to face unbeaten Berlanga on 14 September |url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/boxing/articles/c6p21z6r83jo |access-date=26 July 2024 |publisher=BBC Sport}}{{Cite web |date=2024-07-27 |title=White rips MGM for 'disrespect' amid card conflict |url=https://www.espn.com/boxing/story/_/id/40650416/dana-white-slams-mgm-disrespect-ufc-amid-sept-14-conflict |access-date=2025-01-15 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}} \n\nUFC founder [[Dana White]] had previously attended a concert during [[U2:UV Achtung Baby Live at Sphere|U2's opening residency]] at Sphere and was impressed by the venue's technology, stating that UFC 306 planned to \"take what U2 did and multiply it by a million.\"{{Cite web |last=Weprin |first=Alex |date=2024-09-04 |title=Inside the UFC's $20M Bet on the Las Vegas Sphere: \"Take What U2 Did and Multiply It by a Million\" |url=https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/business/business-news/inside-ufc-las-vegas-sphere-plans-dana-white-1235990615/ |access-date=2025-01-15 |website=The Hollywood Reporter |language=en-US}} Television director [[Glenn Weiss]] and Mexican-American filmmaker [[Carlos López Estrada]] were brought on to oversee the production of the event, including the content that would be displayed on Sphere's [[16K resolution]] video display. The main card was divided into six acts which chronicled the [[history of Mexico]] from the perspective of combat; each act consisted of a 90-second introductory vignette played before each match, and an accompanying \"world\" that served as a backdrop on the screen during the match.{{Cite web |date=2024-09-14 |title=For Mexico, For All Time |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/for-mexico-for-all-time-chapters-riyadh-season-noche-ufc |access-date=2025-01-15 |website=UFC.com |language=en}} \n\nThe use of Sphere resulted in UFC 306 having a higher production cost of around $20 million, in comparison to the approximately $2 million that is spent on other UFC pay-per-view events. UFC would sell a [[Naming rights|title sponsorship]] to an event for the first time, in which UFC 306 was marketed as \"[[Riyadh Season]] Noche UFC\" under an agreement with Saudi Arabia's [[General Entertainment Authority]].{{Cite web |last=Manfre |first=Dylan |date=2024-06-27 |title=UFC, Saudi Arabia Agree to Title Sponsorship of UFC 306 at Sphere |url=https://www.sportico.com/business/sponsorship/2024/ufc-riyadh-season-saudi-arabia-mma-1234785835/ |access-date=2025-01-15 |website=Sportico.com |language=en-US}} Due to the production costs, UFC's long-term agreement with T-Mobile Arena operator [[MGM Resorts International]], and the circumstances that necessitated it, White stated that it was unlikely that UFC would host another Las Vegas event at Sphere, and referred to UFC 306 as an event that \"[would] never be done again\". However, he added that \"if some new technology pops up, I guarantee you I will be first.\" UFC 306 would remain the promotion's most expensive event until 2026, when [[UFC Freedom 250]] was estimated to have exceeded $60 million.{{Cite news |author=Kim Masters |date=2026-02-24 |title=Trump’s UFC math problem and Rob Reiner’s MAGA partner |url=https://puck.news/trumps-ufc-math-problem-rob-reiners-maga-partner/ |access-date=2026-02-25 |publisher=puck.news}}\n\n=== Fight card ===\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]] and [[Merab Dvalishvili]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|date=2024-07-26|title=Noche UFC at Sphere gets Sean O'Malley vs. Merab Dvalishvili title-fight headliner|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/07/ufc-306-sean-omalley-vs-merab-dvalishvili-headlines-sphere-card|access-date=2024-07-26|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] trilogy bout between current champion [[Alexa Grasso]] and former champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] took place at the co-main event.{{Cite web|date=2024-07-26|title=Alexa Grasso vs. Valentina Shevchenko 3 to co-headline Noche UFC at Sphere|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/07/ufc-306-alexa-grasso-vs-valentina-shevchenko-trilogy-booked-sphere|access-date=2024-07-26|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} The pairing first met at [[UFC 285]] in March 2023, where Grasso won the title by fourth round submission upset.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/03/ufc-285-results-alexa-grasso-submits-valentina-shevchenko-rear-naked-choke|title=UFC 285 results: Alexa Grasso makes Valentina Shevchenko tap out, wins flyweight title in huge upset|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2023-03-05|access-date=2023-06-29}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Shevchenko 2]] in September 2023, which the bout ended by split draw.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-09-17|title=Noche UFC: Nail-biter Sees Alexa Grasso vs. Valentina Shevchenko ruled a draw|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/09/17/noche-ufc-alexa-grasso-vs-valentina-shevchenko/|access-date=2023-09-17|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} The pairing previously coached on [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Grasso vs. Team Shevchenko]] against each other.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|title=Season 32 of 'The Ultimate Fighter' set for June with featherweights, middleweights|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Season-32-of-The-Ultimate-Fighter-Set-for-June-With-Featherweights-Middleweights-191964|date=2023-12-07|access-date=2024-03-08|author=Jay Pettry}} [[Manon Fiorot]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/08/ufc-306-manon-fiorot-backup-alexa-grasso-vs-valentina-shevchenko-trilogy|title=Manon Fiorot tabbed as Alexa Grasso-Valentina Shevchenko trilogy backup at UFC 306, coach says|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2024-08-15|access-date=2024-08-15}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Champion|UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Brian Ortega]] and [[Diego Lopes (fighter)|Diego Lopes]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|date=2024-07-26|title=Brian Ortega vs. Diego Lopes rebooked for Noche UFC at Sphere|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/07/ufc-306-brian-ortega-vs-diego-lopes-rebooked-sphere|access-date=2024-07-26|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} The pair was previously expected to meet at [[UFC 303]], but Ortega withdrew just hours before the bout due to illness.{{cite to serve as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/7/26/24207200/michel-pereira-anthony-hernandez-moved-ufc-306-sphere-fight-night-main-event-october|title=Michel Pereira vs. Cháirez]] and Kevin Borjas was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/07/noche-ufc-kevin-borjas-vs-edgar-chairez-added-sphere-las-vegas|title=Kevin Borjas vs. the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by [[Joshua Van]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/09/ufc-306-fight-card-joshua-van-vs-edgar-chairez-kevin-borjas|title=Joshua Van web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc-306/ufc-306-bonus-report-ignacio-bahamondes-one-of-four-fighters-to-take-home-an-extra-50k/|title=UFC 306 Bonus Report: Ignacio Bahamondes one of four fighters to take home an extra $50k|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Chris Taylor|date=2024-09-14|access-date=2024-09-14}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Esteban Ribovics vs. Daniel Zellhuber'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Ignacio Bahamondes''' and '''Ketlen Souza'''\n\n==Records set==\nThe event generated $21,829,245 in ticket receipts, which, at the time, broke the record for the [[List_of_UFC_records#Biggest_gates|biggest gate in UFC history]] surpassing [[UFC 205]] (which previously held the record with a gate of $17,700,000).{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-event-gates-10-highest-ever|title=10 highest UFC event gates in promotion history|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-09-17|access-date=2024-09-18}} The number was later surpassed by [[UFC 329]] in July 2026.{{Cite web|url=https://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/mma-ufc/conor-mcgregor-injured-again-in-ufc-comeback-3849457/|title=Conor McGregor injured again in UFC comeback|publisher=reviewjournal.com|author=Nick Holt|date=2026-07-11|access-date=2026-07-12}}\n\nUFC 306 also now holds highest-grossing records for the following: sales for VIP experiences, merchandise sales at a UFC event, single event ticket sales at the Sphere venue, and sponsorship sales (based on the partnership with [[Riyadh Season]]).{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/9/25/24254125/ufc-306-sets-record-as-highest-grossing-event-in-ufc-history-for-ticket-sales-merchandise-and-more|title=UFC 306 sets record as highest-grossing event in UFC history for ticket sales, merchandise and more|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-09-25|access-date=2024-09-25}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n*[[2024 [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 3, 2024, at the [[Etihad Arena]] in [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/3/6/24092113/ufc-returns-abu-dhabi-fight-night-card-august|title=UFC returns to Abu Dhabi with Fight Night card in August|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2024-03-06|access-date=2024-03-06}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's 19th visit to Abu Dhabi and first since [[UFC 294]] in October 2023.\n\nA bantamweight title eliminator bout between former interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Cory Sandhagen]] and [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-05-06|title=Cory Sandhagen vs. Umar Nurmagomedov booked for main event at UFC Abu Dhabi on Aug. 3|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/5/6/24149968/cory-sandhagen-vs-umar-nurmagomedov-booked-for-main-event-at-ufc-abu-dhabi-on-aug-3|access-date=2024-05-06|publisher=mmafighting.com}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/5/29/24167047/nick-diaz-returns-tony-ferguson-moves-to-welterweight-at-ufc-abu-dhabi|title=Nick Diaz returns, Tony Ferguson moves to welterweight at UFC Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-05-29|access-date=2023-05-29}} The pair was previously expected to headline at [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Font]], but Nurmagomedov withdrew from the event due to a shoulder injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/07/ufc-news-cory-sandhagen-vs-umar-nurmagomedov|title=Umar Nurmagomedov out of UFC on ESPN 50 main Championship|WEC]] and [[List of Strikeforce champions#Welterweight Championship|Strikeforce Welterweight Champion]] [[Nick Diaz]] (also former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger) and [[Vicente Luque]] was expected to take place at the event. However, the bout was removed from the event due to travel issues related to Diaz and the pairing will be postponed to a future date.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Diaz-vs-Luque-Postponed-Shara-Bullet-vs-Oleksiejczuk-Added-to-UFC-Abu-Dhabi-194396|title=Diaz vs. Luque postponed; 'Shara Bullet' vs. Oleksiejczuk added to UFC Abu Dhabi|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2024-07-20|access-date=2024-07-20}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former interim [[UFC Lightweight Champion]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] welterweight winner) [[Tony Ferguson]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Live]] lightweight winner [[Michael Chiesa]] took place at the event. The pair was previously expected to headline at [[UFC Fight Night: McDonald vs. Lineker]], but Chiesa pulled out due to a back injury.{{cite news|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Michael-Chiesa-Injured-Out-of-July-13-UFC-Fight-Night-Headliner-with-Tony-Ferguson-107037|title=Michael Chiesa injured, out of July 13 UFC Fight Night Championship]] that took place on 18 August 2024, at the [[RAC Arena]] in [[Perth]], Australia.{{Cite It marked the first time two African-born athletes met for a title in the organization.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/6/17/24180036/israel-adesanya-announces-dricus-du-plessis-fight-at-ufc-305|title=Israel Adesanya announces Dricus du Plessis fight at UFC 305|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. $6,720,000\n|previousevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Lemos vs. Jandiroba]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC on ABC: Sandhagen vs. Nurmagomedov]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 304: Edwards vs. Muhammad 2''''' was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 27, 2024, at the [[Co-op Live]] in [[Manchester]], England.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/4/24/24138996/ufc-304-will-return-to-manchester-this-summer|title=UFC 304 announced for Manchester this summer|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guillherme Cruz|date=2024-04-24|access-date=2024-04-24}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:The Co-Op Live Arena (geograph 7796386).jpg|thumb|left|This was the first sporting event to take place at the [[Co-op Live]] arena, which opened in April 2024.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/articles/c80z4epzd13o.amp|title=UFC 304 confirmed for Manchester in July|publisher=bbc.com|author=Staff|date=2024-04-24|access-date=2024-04-24}}]] \n\nThe event marked the promotion's fifth visit to Manchester and first since [[UFC 204]] in October 2016. Despite the event taking place in Manchester, the event was held based on [[Pacific Time Zone|Pacific Daylight Time (PDT, UTC-7)]] (Las Vegas) time with preliminary fights starting at 23.00 British Summer Time (15.00 PDT), and the main card starting 03.00 BST, which is the standard start time for UFC pay-per-view events of 19.00 PDT, which drew massive criticism from fans and fighters. Fans believed the matches should have started earlier (19.00 BST, or 11.00 PDT) for the UK audience.{{Cite web|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2024-05-01|title=Aspinall: UFC 304 Start Time 'Absolutely Terrible'|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/ufc/ufc-304-tom-aspinall-start-time|access-date=2024-07-28|publisher=si.com}}\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Leon Edwards]] and [[Belal Muhammad]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Mike Bohn|date=2024-05-16|title=UFC 304 official with Leon Edwards vs. Belal Muhammad headliner and more in Manchester|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/ufc-304-manchester-england-edwards-muhammad-heavyweight|access-date=2024-05-16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} The pair previously fought at [[UFC Fight Night: Edwards vs. Muhammad]] in March 2021, where the fight was declared a no contest following an accidental eye poke which left Muhammad unable to continue.{{Cite web|date=2021-03-14|title=UFC Fight Night 187 results: Leon Edwards vs. Belal Muhammad ends in nasty accidental eye poke|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/ufc-fight-night-187-results-leon-edwards-vs-belal-muhammad-eye-poke-no-contest|access-date=2024-05-16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}}\n\nAn interim [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between the title holder [[Tom Aspinall]] and [[Curtis Blaydes]] co-headlined the event.{{Cite web|date=2024-05-16|title=Interim heavyweight champ Tom Aspinall vs. Curtis Blaydes set for UFC 304 co-main event|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/ufc-304-tom-aspinall-vs-curtis-blaydes-interim-heavyweight-title-co-main-event-manchester-england|access-date=2024-05-16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff}} They previously met at [[UFC Fight Night: Blaydes vs. Aspinall]] in July 2022 in which Blaydes won after Aspinall injured his knee fifteen seconds into the bout keeping him out of competition for a year.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/07/23/ufc-london-curtis-blaydes-vs-tom-aspinall/|title=UFC London: Aspinall suffers knee injury 15 seconds in, Blaydes takes TKO Bryczek|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/05/23/ufc-304-adds-christian-leroy-duncan-vs-robert-bryczek/|access-date=2024-05-24|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} However, Bryczek withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] [[Gregory Rodrigues]].{{Cite news|title=UFC 304: savage middleweight strikers clash at Manchester PPV|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-304-savage-middleweight-strikers-clash-at-manchester-ppv|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 10, 2024, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility, in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-schedule-2024-events-cards-lineups-fights|title=UFC summer schedule mapped out with 11 events in five cities|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-06-24|access-date=2024-06-24}}\n\n==Background==\nA heavyweight rematch between [[Marcin Tybura]] and [[Serghei Spivac]] headlined the event.{{Cite news|title=UFC news: heavyweight rematch set as main event for Las Vegas Fight Night|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-news-heavyweight-rematch-set-as-main-event-for-las-vegas-fight-night-|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2024-07-16|access-date=2024-07-17}} The pair previously met at [[UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo]] in February 2020, which Tybura won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|author=Keith Shillan|date=2020-02-29|title=UFC Norfolk Results: Marcin Tybura beats Sergey Spivak by Decision|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2020/02/29/ufc-norfolk-results-marcin-tybura-uses-heavy-wrestling-gameplan-get-past-sergey-spivak/|access-date=2024-07-17|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Javid Basharat]] and [[Chris Gutiérrez]] was expected to take place a week earlier at [[UFC on ABC: Sandhagen vs. Nurmagomedov]], but it was moved to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|author=Bryson Hester|date=2024-06-19|title=UFC Abu Dhabi adds Chris Gutierrez vs. Javid Basharat|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/06/19/ufc-abu-dhabi-adds-chris-gutierrez-vs-javid-basharat/|access-date=2024-06-20|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/7/11/24195514/morning-report-anthony-smith-ufc-303-loss-probably-puts-the-nail-in-the-coffin-for-title-hopes-mma|title=Morning Report: Anthony Smith sad UFC 303 loss ‘probably puts the nail in the coffin’ for title hopes - Fight Announcements|author=Drake Riggs|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2024-07-11|access-date=2024-07-11}} During fight week, Basharat Nikolay Veretennikov instead.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-bellator-pfl-news-matchup-roundup-fight-bookings-schedule-july-29-2024|title=Matchup Roundup: New UFC, PFL, were cancelled a few days before taking place: a welterweight bout between Yusaku Kinoshita and Jonny Parsons, due to Parsons withdrawing for unknown reasons; and a flyweight bout between [[Allan Nascimento]] and Jafel Filho, due to Nascimento being ill.\n\nAt the weigh-ins, three fighters missed weight:{{Cite pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit.\n\nAll three bouts proceeded at catchweight. Each fighter was fined 20 percent of their individual purses which went to their opponents former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Featherweight Championship|LFA Featherweight Champion]] [[Damon Jackson]], Nikolay Veretennikov, and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger on ESPN+ 105''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 9, 2024, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-schedule-2024-events-dates-locations-fight-card?itm_source=parsely-api|title=Remainder of UFC schedule in 2024 fills out: 15 events across nine cities|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-08-17|access-date=2024-08-17}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the first UFC event held in Nevada since [[cannabis]] was officially removed from the state's list of banned substances. At the beginning of 2024, the Combat Sports Anti-Doping (CSAD) and Drug Free Sport organizations removed cannabis from their banned substances list when the UFC shifted its anti-doping testing from the [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) to CSAD. On October 30, 2024, the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] also announced its decision to remove cannabis from the state's list of prohibited substances. They stated, \"The possession, use, or consumption of cannabis or cannabis products will not be considered an anti-doping violation.\"{{Cite web|author=Staff|date=2024-10-30|title=Marijuana off NSAC naughty list, no longer prohibited for fighters|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/10/29/marijuana-off-nsac-naughty-list-no-longer-prohibited-for-fighters/|access-date=2024-10-30|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] [[Brandon Moreno]] and [[Amir Albazi]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/brandon-moreno/brandon-moreno-ends-brief-time-away-from-mma-set-to-return-at-ufc-vegas-100/|title=Brandon Moreno ends brief time away from MMA, set to return at UFC Vegas 100|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2024-09-02|access-date=2024-09-02}} They were previously expected to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Moreno vs. Royval 2]] earlier in the year but Albazi withdrew due to a neck injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2024/1/11/24035284/ufc-mexico-city-gets-new-headliner-after-amir-albazi-forced-off-card|title=UFC Mexico City gets new headliner after Amir Albazi forced off card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steve Marrocco|date=2024-01-11|access-date=2024-09-02}} However, it was announced a day later than that report that the bout was moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Moreno vs. Albazi]] one week before this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/9/3/24235265/brandon-moreno-vs-amir-albazi-set-as-new-main-event-for-ufc-edmonton|title=Brandon Moreno vs. Amir Albazi set as new main event for UFC Edmonton|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-09-03|access-date=2024-09-03}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Neil Magny]] and [[Carlos Prates]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite previous glove design, which had been replaced at [[UFC 302]] in June. UFC CEO [[Dana White]] cited frequent complaints from fighters over a six-month period.{{Cite web|title=UFC to retire 'new' gloves, permanently return to previous design at UFC 309|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-to-Retire-New-Gloves-Permanently-Return-to-Previous-Design-at-UFC-309-195480|date=2024-11-14|access-date=2024-11-15|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 360\n | image1 = Jon Jones - Supporting Brain Health Study.jpg\n | width1 = 2300 | height1 = 2200\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Stipe Miocic (48086643396) (cropped).jpg\n | width2 = 2300 | height2 = 2200\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer =Jones ''(left)'' made his first heavyweight title defense. event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on January 18, 2025, at [[Intuit Dome]] in [[Inglewood, California]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-announces-2025-schedule-new-events-location-dates|title=UFC announces 2025 schedule including slew of new events|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-11-16|access-date=2024-11-17}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:Intuit Dome Façade.jpg|thumb|left|After hosting 17 events in the [[Greater Los Angeles]] area, this was the promotion's debut at [[Intuit Dome]], which opened in August 2024.]] \n\nThe event marked the promotion's third visit to Inglewood and first since [[UFC 232]] in December 2018. There were concerns regarding the realization of this event due to the [[January 2025 Southern California wildfires|series of recent wildfires]] affecting Southern California, but the venue's organization assured a week before that it would continue as scheduled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-311-continue-as-scheduled-amid-los-angeles-wildfires-intuit-dome|title=Intuit Dome announces UFC 311 will 'continue as scheduled' amid Los Angeles wildfires|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-01-11|access-date=2025-01-11}}\n\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between current lightweight champion [[Islam Makhachev]] and [[Arman Tsarukyan]] was originally scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/ufc-311-main-event-pits-092448436.html|title=UFC 311 main event pits Islam Makhachev against Arman Tsarukyan – with bonus title fight also official|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Alex Pattle|date=2024-12-05|access-date=2024-12-11}} They previously met at [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Oleinik]] in April 2019, where Makhachev defeated Tsarukyan by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/brianmazique/2024/12/04/ufc-311-dana-white-announces-2-huge-championship-fights-for-la-card/|title=UFC 311: Dana White Announces 2 Huge Championship Fights For L.A. Card|work=forbes.com|author=Brian Mazique|date=2024-12-04|access-date=2024-12-11}} One day before the event, it was reported that Tsarukyan suffered an injury that forced him to pull out of the fight. [[Renato Moicano]], who was originally set to face [[Beneil Dariush]] at the same event, stepped in as a replacement for Tsarukyan, and Dariush did not compete at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/1/17/24346063/arman-tsarukyan-out-of-ufc-311-main-event-with-islam-makahchev|title=Arman Tsarukyan out of UFC 311 main event with Islam Makahchev|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2025-01-17|access-date=2025-01-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Merab Dvalishvili]] and undefeated contender [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] served as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/12/ufc-311-news-merab-dvalishvili-vs-umar-nurmagomedov-announced-as-co-main-title-fight|title=UFC 311 news: Merab Dvalishvili vs. Umar Nurmagomedov announced as co-main title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2024-12-04|access-date=2024-12-04}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega]] bantamweight winner Ricky Turcios and Bernardo Sopaj was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Magny vs. Prates]] in November 2024, but it was scrapped after the weigh-ins due to undisclosed medical issues related to Turcios.{{Cite web|date=2024-11-08|title=UFC Fight Night 247 loses another co-main event one day out|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/11/ufc-fight-night-247-ricky-turcios-vs-bernardo-sopaj-canceled-weigh-ins|access-date=2024-11-08|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff}} The pairing was rescheduled to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/11/19/24300864/ricky-turcios-vs-bernardo-sopai-rescheduled-for-ufc-311|title=Ricky Turcios vs. Bernardo Sopai rescheduled for UFC 311|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-11-19|access-date=2024-11-19}}\n\n[[Grant Dawson]] and [[Carlos Diego Ferreira]] are scheduled to compete in a lightweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|title=Grant Dawson to collide with Diego Ferreira at UFC 311 in California on Jan. 18|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Grant-Dawson-to-Collide-With-Diego-Ferreira-at-UFC-311-in-California-on-Jan-18-195530/amp|date=2024-11-21|access-date=2024-11-21}} They were once expected to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Santos vs. Walker]] in October 2021, but Ferreira pulled out due to injury.{{Cite web|date=2021-09-08|author=Matthew Wells|title=Diego Ferreira injured, out of Oct. 2 bout against Grant Dawson|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/09/ufc-news-diego-ferriera-out-of-oct-2-bout-against-grant-dawson-due-to-injury|access-date=2021-09-08|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}\n\nA light [[UFC 312|UFC 312: du Plessis vs Strickland 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Adesanya vs. Imavov''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 250''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 108''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 1, 2025, at the [[ANB Arena|anb Arena]] in [[Riyadh]], Saudi Arabia.{{Cite web|url=https://www.saudimoments.com/a-professional-slapfighting-competition-is-coming-to-riyadh-682883.html|title=A professional slap-fighting contest heads to Riyadh Season|publisher=saudimoments.com|author=Staff|date=2024-09-12|access-date=2024-09-18}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-announces-2025-schedule-new-events-location-dates|title=UFC announces 2025 schedule including slew of new events|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-11-16|access-date=2024-11-17}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to Riyadh and first since [[UFC on ABC: Whittaker vs. Aliskerov]] in June 2024.\n\nA middleweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Israel Adesanya]] and [[Nassourdine Imavov]] headlined this event.{{Cite news|url=https://von.gov.ng/adesanya-vs-imavov-headlines-ufc-riyadh-event/|title=Adesanya vs. Imavov headlines UFC Riyadh event|author=Samuel Chimezie Isaiah|date=2024-11-21|access-date=2024-11-27|archive-date=2024-11-28|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20241128101136/https://von.gov.ng/adesanya-vs-imavov-headlines-ufc-riyadh-event/|url-status=dead|newspaper=[[Voice of Nigeria]]}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Ikram Aliskerov]] and [[André Muniz]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.bg/novini/mma-novini/ikram-aliskerov-sreshtu-andre-munis-prez-fevruari-v-sauditska-arabia#|title=Ikram Aliskerov vs. Andre Munis in February in Saudi Arabia|publisher=mma.bg|author=Staff|date=2024-10-25|access-date=2024-10-27}} {{in lang|bg}} The pair was originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC on ESPN: Perez vs. Taira]] in June 2024, but Muniz withdrew from the bout after he suffered a foot fracture.{{Cite web|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2024-05-30|title=Andre Muniz injured, out of UFC Vegas 93 fight with Ikram Aliskerov|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/5/30/24168085/andre-muniz-injured-ufc-vegas-93-fight-ikram-aliskerov|access-date=2024-05-30|publisher=mmafighting.com}} In turn, the bout was cancelled once again and was rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Hill vs. Rountree Jr.]] as Muniz and his team had visa issues.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-saudi-arabia-fight-card-cancellation-andre-muniz-vs-ikram-aliskerov|title=UFC Fight Night 250 loses main card fight over passport issues|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-01-26|access-date=2025-01-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/ufc-middleweight-aliskerov-i-have-a-fight-with-muniz-on-april-26/|title=UFC Middleweight Aliskerov: I Have a Fight with Muniz on April 26|publisher=telecomasia.net|author=Marina Magomedova|date=2025-02-02|access-date=2025-02-02}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Jordan Leavitt]] and Abdul-Kareem Al-Selwady was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-12-23|title=UFC Saudi Arabia full fight card revealed with Israel Adesanya vs. Nassourdine Imavov in main event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/12/23/24328161/ufc-saudi-arabia-full-fight-card-revealed-with-israel-adesanya-vs-nassourdine-imavov-in-main-event|access-date=2025-01-10|publisher=mmafighting.com}} However, Leavitt withdrew for unknown reasons and was replaced by Bolaji Oki.{{Cite web|date=2025-01-10|title=Bolaji Oki replaces Jordan Leavitt at UFC Saudi Arabia vs. Abdul-Kareem Al-Selwady|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-news-bolaji-oki-vs-abdul-kareem-al-selwady-jordan-leavitt|access-date=2025-01-10|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun}} In turn, one week prior to the event, Oki withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons, so the bout was subsequently cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-saudi-arabia-fight-card-abdul-kareem-alselwady-vs-bolaji-oki|title=UFC Saudi Arabia loses another fight days prior to event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-01-27|access-date=2025-01-27}}\n\nA lightweight rematch between former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Lightweight championship|KSW Lightweight]] and [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Featherweight championship|Featherweight Champion]] [[Mateusz Gamrot]] and [[Rafael Fiziev]] was reportedly linked to this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/12/12/24319942/report-mateusz-gamrot-and-rafael-fiziev-run-it-back-at-ufc-saudi-arabia-in-2025|title=Report: Mateusz Gamrot and Rafael Fiziev run it back at UFC They previously headlined [[UFC Fight Night: Fiziev vs. Gamrot]] in September 2023, where Gamrot won due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/09/23/ufc-vegas-79-rafael-fiziev-vs-mateusz-gamrot/|title=Rafael Fiziev knee injury ends UFC Vegas 79 main event early, Mateusz Gamrot awarded TKO web|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/brianmazique/2025/01/24/ufc-legend-and-future-hall-of-famer-has-signed-with-new-promotion/|title=UFC legend and future hall-of-famer has signed with new promotion|publisher=forbes.com|author=Brian Mazique|date=2025-01-24|access-date=2025-01-24}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Lucas Alexander weighed in at 148.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight with Alexander forfeiting 30% of his purse which went to his opponent Bogdan Grad.{{Cite web|date=2025-01-31|title=UFC Fight Night 250 weigh-in results: Adesanya vs. mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 15, 2025, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-announces-2025-schedule-new-events-location-dates|title=UFC announces 2025 schedule including slew of new events|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-11-16|access-date=2024-11-16}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Jared Cannonier]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] [[Gregory Rodrigues]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Craig Pekios|date=2024-12-03|title='Robocop' books first UFC main event against Jared Cannonier on February 15|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/robocop-books-first-ufc-main-event/|accessdate=2024-12-04|publisher=lowkickmma.com}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Peña vs. Team Nunes]] flyweight winner Juliana Miller and Carli Judice was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2024-11-24|title=UFC matchmaking bulletin: fights added to Los Angeles-held UFC 311|url=https://www.mmanews.com/features/matchmaking-bulletin/fights-added-ufc-311/|access-date=2024-11-24|publisher=mmanews.com}} However, Miller withdrew from the fight due to an injury, so the bout was scrapped and Judice was booked for another event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/12/ufc-news-yuneisy-duben-vs-carli-judice-booked-dana-white|title=Yuneisy Duben books UFC debut vs. Carli Judice after stunning Dana White's Contender Series knockout|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-12-23|access-date=2024-12-23}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Jacob Malkoun]] and [[Rodolfo Vieira]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Jacob Malkun vs. Rodolfo Vieira at UFC Vegas 102|url=https://mma.bg/novini/mma-novini/dzheykab-malkun-sreshtu-rodolfo-vieyra-na-ufc-vegas-102|date=2024-11-22|access-date=2024-11-22|publisher=mma.bg|author=MMA Bulgaria}} {{in lang|bg}} However, Malkoun withdrew from the fight due to an undisclosed injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/12/ufc-fight-night-251-news-jacob-malkoun-injury-out-rodolfo-vieira|title=UFC Fight on ESPN: Covington vs. Buckley]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 311|UFC 311: Makhachev vs. Moicano]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Dern vs. Ribas 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 249, UFC Vegas 101''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 107''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] taking place on January 11, 2025, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-announces-2025-schedule-new-events-location-dates|title=UFC announces 2025 schedule including slew of new events|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-11-16|access-date=2024-11-17}}\n\n==Background==\nA women's strawweight rematch between [[Mackenzie Dern]] and [[Amanda Ribas]] headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/12/3/24312526/swerve-mackenzie-dern-vs-amanda-ribas-2-promoted-to-ufc-vegas-101-main-event-not-ufc-tampa|title=Swerve! Mackenzie Dern vs. Amanda Ribas 2 promoted to UFC Vegas 101 main event (not UFC Tampa)|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2024-12-03|access-date=2024-12-03}} They were originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC on ESPN: Covington vs. Buckley]] on December 14, 2024 but the bout was moved to this event in order to serve as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Report-Amanda-RibasMackenzie-Dern-Rematch-Set-for-UFC-Tampa-on-Dec-14-194688|title=Report: Amanda Ribas-Mackenzie Dern rematch set for UFC Tampa on Dec. 14|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Sayan Nag|date=2024-08-19|access-date=2024-08-19}} The duo previously met at [[UFC Fight Night: Joanna vs. Waterson]] in October 2019, where Ribas won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2019/10/12/ufc-tampa-results-amanda-ribas/|title=UFC Tampa Results: Amanda Ribas dominates Mackenzie Dern|author=Josh Evanoff|date=2019-10-12|publisher=cagesidepress.com|access-date=2019-10-13}} \n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[List of UFC champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Germaine de Randamie]] and former [[Legacy_Fighting_Alliance#LFA_Women's_Bantamweight_Championship|LFA Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Jacqueline Cavalcanti]] was reportedly scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Jacqueline Cavalcanti faces former champion on the first UFC card in 2025|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/jacqueline-cavalcanti-enfrenta-ex-campea-no-primeiro-card-do-ufc-em-2025/|date=2024-10-25|access-date=2024-10-25|publisher=agfight.com|author=Diego Ribas}} {{in lang|pt-br}} However, de Randamie announced her retirement and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/10/ufc-news-germaine-de-randamie-announces-retirement|title=Former UFC champion Germaine de Randamie announces retirement from MMA|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2024-10-31|access-date=2024-10-31}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/11/6/24288703/morning-report-michael-chandler-warned-jon-jones-im-coming-to-steal-the-show-at-ufc-309|title=Morning Report: Michael Chandler warned Jon Jones ‘I’m coming to steal the show’ at UFC 309|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2024-11-06|access-date=2024-11-06}}\n\nA welterweight bout between Preston Parsons and Andreas Gustafsson was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2024-10-15|title=Preston Parsons meets Andreas Gustafsson at UFC's January 11 Fight Night|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/10/15/preston-parsons-meets-andreas-gustafsson-at-ufcs-january-11-fight-night/|access-date=2025-01-04|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} However, Gustafsson withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by Jacobe Smith.{{Cite web|date=2025-01-04|title=Jacobe Smith slides into UFC debut on short notice vs. Preston Parsons|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-news-jacobe-smith-debut-vs-preston-parsons|access-date=2025-01-04|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn}}\n\nIn addition, [[Nurullo Aliev]] and Yanal Ashmouz were scheduled to meet in a lightweight bout. However, Ashmouz withdrew for unknown reasons and was replaced by [[Joe Solecki]].{{Cite web|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2025-01-04|title=UFC Vegas 101 sees 2 bouts changed a week from event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/1/4/24335865/ufc-vegas-101-sees-two-bouts-changed-a-week-from-event|access-date=2025-01-05|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Jose Johnson and Ihor Potieria missed weight. Johnson weighed in at 128.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. Potieria weighed in at 188 pounds, two pounds over the middleweight non-title fight limit. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight, with Johnson forfeiting 20% of his purse and Potieria forfeiting 25% of his purse, which went to their opponents former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Flyweight Championship|LFA Flyweight Champion]] Felipe Bunes and Marco Tulio, respectively.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|title=UFC Vegas 101 weigh-in results: main event set; 2 fighters the world stage|publisher=nsw.gov.au|author=NSW Government|date=2024-10-17|access-date=2024-10-17}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's seventh visit to Sydney and first since [[UFC 293]] in September 2023.\n\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Dricus du Plessis]] (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Welterweight championship|KSW Welterweight Champion]]) and former champion [[Sean Strickland]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-announces-2-huge-title-fights-for-ufc-312|title=UFC announces 2 huge title fights for UFC 312|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2024-12-07|access-date=2024-12-07}} The pairing previously met one year prior at [[UFC 297]] in which du Plessis captured the title by split decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/21/24045654/dricus-du-plessis-dethrones-sean-strickland-win-title-ufc-297-main-event-calls-out-israel-adesanya|title=Dricus du Plessis dethrones Sean Strickland to win title in UFC 297 main event, calls out Israel Adesanya|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-01-21|access-date=2024-12-07}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current two-time champion [[Zhang Weili]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia]] strawweight winner [[Tatiana Suarez]] served as the co-main event.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Jimmy Crute]] and [[Marcin Prachnio]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/11/ufc-312-first-three-bouts-announced|title=First three bouts announced for UFC 312 in Australia|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2024-11-11|access-date=2025-01-07}} However, Prachnio withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by former [[Legacy_Fighting_Alliance#LFA_Light_Heavyweight_Championship|LFA Light Heavyweight Champion]] Rodolfo Bellato.{{Cite web|title=Unofficially: Marcin Prachnio will not appear at UFC 312, Jimmy Crute rival|url=https://www.lowking.pl/nieoficjalnie-marcin-prachnio-nie-wystapi-na-ufc-312-jimmy-crute-z-nowym-rywalem/|date=2025-01-06|access-date=2025-01-07|publisher=lowking.pl|author=Bartłomiej $3,841,751.94\n|previousevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Cannonier vs. Rodrigues]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Kape vs. Almabayev]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Song''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 252''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 110''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 22, 2025, at the [[Climate Pledge Arena]] in [[Seattle, Washington]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-announces-2025-schedule-new-events-location-dates|title=UFC announces 2025 schedule including slew of new events|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-11-16|access-date=2024-11-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's fourth visit to Seattle and first since [[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Moraga]] in July 2013.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/12/ufc-seattle-fight-card-february-22-2025-climate-pledge-arena|title=UFC makes Seattle return official for early 2025|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-12-07|access-date=2024-12-07}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Champion]] (also [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]]) [[Henry Cejudo]] and [[Song Yadong]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2025-01-06|title=Henry Cejudo vs. Song Yadong headlines UFC Seattle, Dominick Cruz returns in co-main event|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/ufc/cejudo-song-yadong-ufc-seattle-dom-cruz/|access-date=2025-01-06|publisher=mmanews.com}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Ketlen Vieira]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] women's featherweight winner [[Macy Chiasson]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|title=Macy Chiasson vs. Ketlen Vieira set for UFC event on Feb. 22|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Macy-Chiasson-vs-Ketlen-Vieira-Set-for-UFC-Event-on-Feb-22-195402|date=2024-11-05|access-date=2024-11-06|author=Tristen Critchfield}} However, due to an injury suffered by Chiasson, the bout was removed from the card.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Seattle-Lineup-Shifts-Due-to-Injury-New-Added-Matches-196154|title=UPDATED UFC Seattle Lineup Shifts Due to Injury, New Added Matches|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2024-12-13|access-date=2025-02-13}} The pair was previously scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Ankalaev vs. Walker 2]] in January 2024, but Vieira was pulled as a result of an injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-12-30|title=Loss of Macy Chiasson vs. Ketlen Vieira among hits to UFC Vegas 84|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/12/30/loss-macy-chiasson-ketlen-vieira-ufc-vegas-84/|access-date=2024-06-17|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}\n\nA middleweight bout between Mansur Abdul-Malik and Antonio Trócoli was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|date=2025-01-03|title=UFC Seattle adds Mansur Abdul-Malik vs. Antonio Trocoli|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-seattle-adds-mansur-abdul-malik-vs-antonio-trocoli|access-date=2025-01-25|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} However, Trócoli withdrew for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by Nick Klein.{{Cite web|date=2025-01-25|title=Nick Klein steps into short-notice debut at UFC Fight Night 252 vs. Mansur Abdul-Malik|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/01/ufc-seattle-nick-klein-vs-mansur-abdul-malik|access-date=2025-01-25|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}}\n\nA welterweight bout between promotional newcomer [[Islam Dulatov]] and [[Adam Fugitt]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/12/03/adam-fugitt-vs-islam-dulatov-lands-on-ufc-seattle/|title=Adam Fugitt vs. Islam Dulatov lands on UFC Seattle|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2024-12-03|access-date=2025-01-28}} However, Dulatov withdrew from the fight due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by Billy Goff.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/01/28/islam-dulatov-out-of-ufc-debut-on-feb-22-billy-goff-in/|title=Islam Dulatov out of UFC debut on Feb. 22, Billy Goff in|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-01-28|access-date=2025-01-28}} In turn, the bout was scrapped during fight week as Fugitt pulled out due to injury.{{Cite
